# Mastercard Developers
Mastercard Developers is a comprehensive API platform that provides issuers, acquirers, financial services companies, fintech, and developers with access to Mastercard products and services. The platform offers APIs for payments, fraud prevention, identity verification, open finance, loyalty management, digital commerce, and more. Developers can integrate these APIs to build modern financial experiences while leveraging Mastercard's security infrastructure and global reach.

## Products
- [Account Services Catalogue](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/account-services-catalogue/): Receives Universal Specification, routes to Service Providers for processing and ensures a timely response to submitter.
- [Account to Account Commerce](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/account-to-account-commerce/): Account to Account Commerce enable Creditor Service Providers and Debtor Service Providers to offer their customers with seamless and efficient mobile payment journeys.
- [Authentication Solutions](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/authentication-solutions/): Providing secure, seamless authentication solutions
- [Automatic Billing Updater (ABU)](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/automatic-billing-updater-abu/): Reduce card-not-present transaction declines caused by changed account numbers and expiration dates
- [BIN Lookup](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/bin-lookup/): An API that contains up-to-date active Mastercard valid issuing account ranges that can be used for authorization routing, fraud prevention, and other novel solutions such as loyalty checks.
- [BS Creditor API](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/bs-creditor-api/): Automatic payment system for Danish customers
- [Benefit Allocation Service](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/benefit-allocations/): Enable issuers to segment and personalize benefits per card
- [Benefits Content Eligibility Service](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/benefits-content-eligibility-service/): Enable the presentation of Mastercard Benefits Content to consumers
- [Benefits Eligibility Service](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/benefits-eligibility/): Enable cardholders eligibility to retrieve their benefits and to personalize their experiences
- [Bill Pay](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/bill-pay/): Provide a mobile-first solution, adding the power of real-time capabilities to Mastercard’s industry-leading biller network
- [Bill Payment Validator](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/bill-payment-validator/): Allows customers to check if an account is eligible for bill payment via RPPS.
- [Carbon Calculator](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/carbon-calculator/): Services designed to calculate carbon emitted by transactions
- [Carbon Calculator Experience](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/carbon-calculator-experience/): A seamless service to help consumers visualize their carbon footprint and donate to offset their emissions
- [Click to Pay](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/click-to-pay/): Enable faster, secure, and smarter digital network checkout experience.
- [Commercial Direct Payments](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/commercial-direct-payments/): Reduce complexity and risk, cut costs, and automate Business-to-Business (B2B) card payments processes around the world
- [Commercial Event Notifications](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/commercial-event-notifications/): Efficiently process B2B payments with rapid payment and token notifications for accurate reconciliation
- [Community Pass](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/community-pass/): An interoperable digital platform facilitating sustainable service delivery for marginalized individuals and communities
- [Consumer Credit Analytics](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/consumer-credit-analytics/): A suite of data product solutions that provides near-real-time scores and insights to Lenders to augment credit decisioning utilizing alternate data sources.
- [Currency Conversion Calculator](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/currency-conversion-calculator/): Retrieve Mastercard's foreign exchange rates instantly
- [Doconomy Aland Index](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/doconomy-aland-index/): Provide your users with actionable sustainability services
- [Dynamic Yield](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/dynamic-yield/): Realize the full power of tomorrow’s personalization, today. Build irresistibly personalized, optimized, and synchronized digital customer experiences faster and easier than ever before.
- [Ethoca Alerts](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/ethoca-alerts-for-merchants/): Connects issuers, acquirers and merchants to share fraud and dispute data, speeding up the dispute resolution process. By receiving real-time fraud alerts, merchants can quickly identify fraud, stop order fulfillment, issue refunds, and prevent the need for chargebacks.
- [Ethoca Consumer Clarity™](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/ethoca-consumer-digital-experience/): Share merchant details and digital receipts via cardholder banking app. Identify subscription transactions and enable subscription management capabilities. Reduce disputes. Elevate experience.
- [First-Party Trust](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/first-party-trust/): A service that provides a liability shift for merchants that can supply compelling evidence of first-party fraud.
- [Flight Delay Pass](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/flight-delay-pass/): Flight Delay Pass is a great alternative to a full access airport lounge program for products with lower economics. It delivers the value of an airport lounge benefit with a lower price point and provides peace of mind to cardholders seeking a clean, safe alternative to crowded terminals.
- [Fraud and Loss Database (FLD)](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/fld-fraud-submission/): Report & Manage Fraud transactions to Mastercard
- [Identity Insights for Accounts](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/identity-insights-for-accounts/): Mastercard Identity Insights for Accounts empowers businesses to enable frictionless commerce for consumers while reducing fraud and improving confidence in account authentication and authorization.
- [Identity Insights for Transactions](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/identity-insights-for-transactions/): Mastercard Identity Insights for Transactions empowers businesses to enable frictionless commerce for consumers while reducing fraud and improving confidence in transaction authentication and authorization.
- [In Control for Commercial Payments](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/in-control-for-commercial-payments/): Mastercard In Control for Commercial Payments™ (ICCP) streamlines commercial payments by leveraging virtual cards with configurable spending controls, and enhanced data.
- [Installments](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/installments/): Giving consumers the choice and flexibility to split the cost of purchases into installments
- [Location Intelligence](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/location-intelligence/): Access Mastercard's global database of ATM and Mastercard easySavings locations to use in your app or website.
- [MATCH Pro](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/match/): Help acquirers identify potentially high-risk merchants before entering to a merchant agreement.
- [MCBP MP SDK](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mobile-payments-sdk/): Simplifies the development of Mastercard Cloud-Based Payments (MCBP) applications for Android and iOS devices.
- [MDES](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mdes/): Provides services around the tokenization of Mastercard card numbers for increased payment security.
- [Mastercard Account Validation](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-account-validation/): Checks cardholder names with card issuers in real time
- [Mastercard Benefits & Experiences Portal](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-benefits-and-experiences-portal/): Enable cardholders to see and personalize their card benefits to best suit their lifestyle needs
- [Mastercard Cloud Commerce](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/cloud-commerce/): Develop simple physical and digital merchant acceptance with Mastercard cloud native software-based acceptance solution.
- [Mastercard Commercial Connect API](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/commercial-connect-api/): Mastercard Commercial Connect API acts as the point of access for partners to access all of Mastercard's commercial API capabilities, creating a streamlined integration process and a unified developer experience across business workflows.
- [Mastercard Contactless Reader SDK](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/tap-on-phone-terminal-sdk/): Turn an off- the-shelf NFC-enabled android device, into an acceptance device
- [Mastercard Cross-Border Services](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/cross-border-services/): Enabling banks, people, and businesses to make frictionless cross-border payments with certainty and predictability.
- [Mastercard Developer Hub for FDX APIs](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/fdx-developer-hub/): Best-in-class tools to create FDX-compliant APIs
- [Mastercard Donate](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-donate/): Increase your social impact easily by integrating Mastercard Donate in your website or mobile app
- [Mastercard Easy Savings Program](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-easy-savings-program/): Enable issuers to embed global offers programs designed for SMEs, driving top-of-wallet spend behavior.
- [Mastercard Gateway](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-gateway/): Provides multi-channel global payment processing services and advanced fraud prevention and risk management solutions to merchants and banks.
- [Mastercard Insurance Programs](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-insurance-programs/): Mastercard Insurance Programs are APIs tailored to cardholders' needs when filing a claim - providing convenience and accessibility all in one place.
- [Mastercard Loyalty Management](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-loyalty-management/): Enable valuable functions for your end-to-end rewards program
- [Mastercard Loyalty Promotions](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-loyalty-promotions/): Engage and reward customers with customized campaigns delivered via a seamless digital experience
- [Mastercard Move](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/move/): A portfolio of global money movement capabilities powering a variety of payment experiences, including personal payments, business payments and disbursements
- [Mastercard Pay with Rewards™](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-pay-with-rewards/): Consumers can easily redeem their rewards points by paying with Mastercard
- [Mastercard Processing](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-processing/): Powering card management for banks and fintech companies interested in prepaid, debit, and credit products.
- [Mastercard Redemption Services](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-redemption-services/): Boost consumer engagement and spend with a variety of market-leading digital redemption options.
- [Mastercard Send™](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-send/): Enables everyday commerce through fast, convenient and secure funds transfers, domestically and cross border
- [Mastercard Sonic Branding](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-sonic-branding/): The unique set of 6 notes and animation that reinforces peace of mind through a multisensory confirmation.
- [Mastercard Track™ Business Payment Service](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-track-business-payment-service/): Create an open-loop network of partners and process card payments.
- [Mastercard Virtual Card Tokens](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercard-virtual-card-tokens/): Tokenize browser autofill transactions for an enhanced checkout experience.
- [Mastercard® Identity Check™](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/identity-check/): Improve security without sacrificing the customer experience with Mastercard Identity Check
- [Mastercom®](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/mastercom/): Expedite dispute resolution and protect your business
- [Merchant Cloud](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/merchant-cloud/): With Merchant Cloud, we’re powering tomorrow’s growth by connecting the dots today, bringing industry-leading commerce solutions onto a single, open platform.
- [Offers Merchant Content](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/eop-admin/): Enable external partners - such as merchants and aggregators - to seamlessly push content, including merchant profiles, offers, and images, to the Offers platform.
- [Offers for Publishers](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/personalized-offers/): Drive top-of-wallet preference, incremental spend, and unparalleled engagement with merchant-funded offers.
- [Onboard Risk Check](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/onboard-risk-check/): Onboard Risk Check (ORC) is a comprehensive solution designed to help customers make more informed decisions during merchant onboarding.
- [Open Finance (Europe)](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/open-finance-europe/): Connect your applications to ~3,000 European banks to access financial data and enable seamless payments
- [Open Finance (US & AU)](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/open-finance/): Easily connect your customers’ financial data to your product.
- [Pay by Account](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/pay-by-account/): Offer consumers a convenient and secure way to pay from any account, using the existing mobile banking app
- [Places](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/places/): Access Mastercard's global database of merchant locations to make your products more insightful and intelligent.
- [Priceless Platform](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/priceless-platform/): Tap into the world of Priceless experiences, offers, and benefits and publish them on your digital platforms
- [Priceless Specials](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/priceless-specials/): Deliver a wide range of merchant offers to Mastercard cardholders via your mobile apps or websites
- [RiskRecon API](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/riskrecon/): Cybersecurity ratings and insights that make it easy to understand and act on your risks.
- [Small Business Credit Analytics](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/small-business-credit-analytics/): Access near real-time spend data from Mastercard-accepting small businesses to improve credit decisions.
- [Subscription Controls](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/subscription-controls/): Offer cardholders an integrated digital experience to manage their subscriptions – improving payment acceptance, boosting top-of-wallet preference and avoiding write-offs
- [Token Authentication Service (TAS)](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/token-authentication-service/): Enable passkeys to seamlessly authenticate cardholders in remote commerce transactions
- [Track Search](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/track-search/): Enhance your commercial payment program capabilities using enhanced Mastercard merchant acceptance and Mastercard Track Business Payment Service data.
- [Transaction Notifications](https://developer.mastercard.com/product/transaction-notifications/): The Transaction Notification API makes it easier than ever for consumers to share their purchase alerts with a third-party app or service allowing them to receive real-time notifications and offer just-in-time experiences to cardholders.

## Services

### Account to Account Commerce for Creditor Service Providers
serviceId: account-to-account-commerce-for-csp
authType: MTLS
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/account-to-account-commerce-for-csp/documentation/index.md

### Account to Account Commerce for Debtor Service providers
serviceId: account-to-account-commerce-for-dsp
authType: MTLS
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/account-to-account-commerce-for-dsp/documentation/index.md

### Mastercard Account Validation
serviceId: account-validation
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/account-validation/documentation/index.md

### Authentication Facilitator
serviceId: authentication-facilitator
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/authentication-facilitator/documentation/index.md

Overview: The Authentication Facilitator API is a component of the Mastercard Authentication Service that enables MDES token requestors—such as digital wallets and commerce platforms—to authenticate cardholders using information maintained by the card issuer. It supports outbound web requests that notify issuers to deliver a Mastercard-generated authentication code to the cardholder. This code is then entered by the cardholder into the token requestor’s interface to complete authentication. The API is designed to work with any MDES token type and token requestor program, regardless of the card product or context of use, and is available only in approved business scenarios.

Why It Matters: The Authentication Facilitator API provides a secure and standardized way for token requestors to verify cardholder identity, enabling new digital use cases that require authentication beyond initial token provisioning. It helps issuers maintain control over authentication flows while supporting seamless consumer experiences. The service ensures privacy and security through mutually authenticated TLS and payload encryption, and is compliant with PCI DSS standards. By leveraging issuer-held cardholder data, the API supports flexible, scalable authentication across the MDES ecosystem.

- [Authentication Facilitator](https://developer.mastercard.com/authentication-facilitator/documentation/index.md)

### Automatic Billing Updater (ABU)
serviceId: automatic-billing-updater
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/automatic-billing-updater/documentation/index.md

Overview: Automatic Billing Updater (ABU) is one of the key security APIs on the Mastercard Developers Platform. Streamlining account lifestyle management for credential-on-file and recurring payment, ABU maintains a global repository of card account lifecycle events provided by issuers - such as new account creation, credential changes (ex: expiry updates or replacements), closures, brand flips, and portfolio upgrades. To obtain these lifecycle updates, ABU supports two integration models: a Push subscription model that delivers update notifications for accounts you subscribe to watch, and a Pull inquiry model for on‑demand checks via API or daily batch files.

Why it matters: Outdated credentials can cause a plethora of problems, both on the issuer and user side like declines, checkout friction, cart abandonment, and subscription disruptions.  ABU enables validation of current credentials before processing scheduled payments and supports subscribing new customer accounts for future change notifications. The push model is especially effective when future transaction timing is unpredictable, while the pull model works well as a fallback after a decline or as a proactive, periodic check to maintain account accuracy. 

- [Automatic Billing Updater](https://developer.mastercard.com/automatic-billing-updater/documentation/index.md)

### BIN Lookup
serviceId: bin-lookup
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/bin-lookup/documentation/index.md

Overview: BIN Lookup provides accurate, up-to-date bank identification number (BIN) and account range data to support decisions across the payments ecosystem. Partners are required to request and download the complete dataset at least every 6 days and deliver refreshed data to their customers daily, ensuring the most current issuer updates are reflected. The API supports unlimited calls, enabling seamless access to the latest BIN data as frequently as needed.n nWhy it matters: This API helps applications improve authorization routing, detect fraud, and support compliance and operational use cases by identifying card attributes such as brand, product type, issuer name, country code, and ICA number. It is particularly valuable for payment processors, acquirers, and merchants who need to make real-time decisions based on card characteristics.

### Commercial Event Notifications
serviceId: commercial-event-notifications
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/commercial-event-notifications/documentation/index.md

Overview: The commercial event notifications service generates and delivers notifications for events of interest to subscribers in the area of commercial payments using virtual card numbers. Subscribers can be issuers, companies, or agents who create VCNs to make business payments. Working in partnership with the In Control for Mobile Payments API, the commercial event notifications api delivers token events for VCNs generated by In Control for Commercial Payments or Business Payment Controls. Once setup, you can subscribe to receive notifications with the filter conditions for events you care about, across multiple subject clearing events. Why it matters:  Commercial event notifications empowers businesses with real-time insights into Virtual Card Network activity. Among other things, the Commercial Event Notifications can trigger immediate alerts for suspicious VCN activity, improve visibility on VCN usage patterns, stream reconciliation processes by having real-time transaction data, automate workflows, etc. Notifications are delivered via HTTPS POST and may batch multiple notifications (from the same or different subscriptions) into a single request; the endpoint must handle JSON, support TLS/mTLS, and de-duplicate potential repeat deliveries.

### Consents
serviceId: consent-management
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/consent-management/documentation/index.md

Overview:Consent Management is a flexible Mastercard service that enables partners to securely capture, manage, and verify cardholder consent for a wide range of data-sharing use cases. Designed to support both direct-to-consumer, third-party mediated and agentic consent flows. The service ensures that consent is explicit, traceable, and compliant with privacy and security standards.Whether you're a fintech, issuer, merchant, or data aggregator, Consent Management helps you build trust with users while unlocking access to permissioned transaction data. Why Use Consent Management?nPortability across use cases: Use the same standardized consent infrastructure to support multiple services - from real-time notifications to personalized offers, financial insights, and more.Flexible integration: Choose between Mastercard-hosted UI (ideal for PCI-free teams) or direct API integration (for PCI-compliant environments).Trust and transparency: Deliver a secure, branded experience that clearly communicates terms, privacy policies, and user control.Core Functionality Enrollment models:Consent lifecycle management: Supports expiration, renewal, revocation, and deletion via APIs Authentication support: Includes strong cardholder verification using 3D Secure (3DS) and Account Status Inquiry (ASI) Single-card enrollment via hosted UI or APIs Bulk enrollment for partners with pre-obtained consent (e.g., via cardholder agreements)

Keywords: Consent management solution, Consent management platform, PCI-compliant consent mechanism, Authenticated Consent, User permissioned data sharing

### Consumer Clarity
serviceId: consumer-clarity
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/consumer-clarity/documentation/index.md

Overview: Consumer Clarity is an API that enriches cardholder transaction experiences and supports issuer operations with clear, real-time merchant and purchase details. It enables banking apps and call center teams to retrieve recognizable merchant names and logos, locations, and additional merchant context. The service also provides Ethoca-generated digital receipts (when the merchant participates in the Ethoca network), and subscription information with centralized management actions. All capabilities are accessible through the Consumer Clarity API (formerly the Enriched Transactions API).
Purpose and core functionality: In digital channels, Consumer Clarity delivers on-demand enrichment for transaction lists and details, helping cardholders recognize purchases, view available digital receipts, and manage subscriptions (for example, cancel, pause, upgrade) directly with merchants. In call centers, it returns real-time merchant and transaction data—such as order details, refunds, and merchant contact information—and, when applicable, First-Party Trust transaction data with compelling evidence, available via a secure URL, raw JSON, or a web-based UI.
Why it matters: By providing purchase transparency and context on demand, Consumer Clarity helps cardholders resolve confusion about transactions in real-time. For issuers, the richer data equips staff to make informed decisions and can eliminate costly, unnecessary chargebacks stemming from first-party fraud and customer disputes. The combined digital and call center capabilities streamline resolution, improve customer experience, and reduce operational overhead.

### Mastercard Cross-Border Services
serviceId: cross-border-services
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/cross-border-services/documentation/index.md

Overview: Cross Border Services is a global payment service that provides access to faster, transparent payments between Senders and Recipients through multiple channels and payout methods. Mastercard Cross Border's supported services include the following: a technical system interface to initiate transactions, wholesale and retail foreign exchange rates and fees, customer support, settlement services between the customer and Mastercard, pricing and rate mechanism to determine up-front pricing information. Payment transfer types include the following: person-to-person (P2P), person-to-business (P2B), business-to-business (B2B), and business-to-person (B2P). Why it matters: The financial services industry faces growing global demand for more practical, digital solutions for cross-border payments. Cross-Border Services offers solutions for the evolving world of work, commerce and technologies, changing how money moves – both within countries and across borders – and fueling the need for a simple, scalable, trusted way for people and businesses to transact with confidence.  Customers are able to integrate with Cross-Border Services through APIs, payment files or SWIFT.

### Standard Currency Conversion Calculator
serviceId: currency-conversion-calculator
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/currency-conversion-calculator/documentation/index.md

Overview: The Standard Currency Conversion Calculator is a service for issuers to access Mastercard’s daily currency conversion rates and calculate cardholder billing amounts for card transactions. It supports building mobile or web experiences where cardholders can see the applicable conversion rate and billed amount based on their transaction details. Rates are published daily and are effective for 24 hours. The service is available in NA, LAC, MEA, Europe (excluding EEA), and AP; issuers in the EEA should use the Enhanced Currency Conversion Calculator API. Why it matters: This helps applications precisely determine conversion results for payment scenarios, verify the availability of the latest rates, and understand which currencies are supported. It supports use cases such as international peer-to-peer payments, travel expense tracking, e-commerce pricing, and banking transparency. By using Mastercard’s rates, the API ensures consistency with actual billing and helps users understand the financial impact of currency conversion. 

Keywords: currency conversion, fx rates, mastercard fx rates, currency conversion api, fx rates api

- [Standard Currency Conversion Calculator](https://developer.mastercard.com/currency-conversion-calculator/documentation/index.md)
- [API Basics](https://developer.mastercard.com/currency-conversion-calculator/documentation/api-basics/index.md)
- [API Reference](https://developer.mastercard.com/currency-conversion-calculator/documentation/api-reference/index.md)

### Mastercard Digital Redemptions
serviceId: digital-redemptions
authType: OAuth1.0a and OAuth2 (FAPI 2.0)
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/digital-redemptions/documentation/index.md

Digital Redemptions is an API that allows Digital Merchants or Issuers to offer points redemption within their checkout, for purchases made by members of the participating issuers rewards program. It enables cardholders (“Redeemers”) in participating Rewards Programs to convert rewards points into eligible products, services, or cash. The service supports creating redemptions by specifying points, cash value (with Mastercard calculating points), or both (with validation to ensure alignment) The digital redemptions API, if integrated by a merchant/issuer, allows consumers to redeem points directly on a merchant's website. Additionally, it can be used for data access for merchants, integration with a merchant's checkout for a seamless rewards experience, and can be used to customize the consumer experience.Why it matters: Digital Redemptions allows merchants and issuers to consolidate the system they use to offer loyalty points. Digital Redemptions streamlines rewards redemption at checkout by confirming account eligibility and good standing, and by handling the points-to-cash value conversion without requiring merchants to manage rewards logic or balance sources.Through the API, the customer can perform a variety of different actions. These include: searching/getting the profile of a points' redeemer, create/manage redemptions, reverse redemptions, update redemption status, get/create shipping address for redemptions, etc.

### Benefits Eligibility Service
serviceId: eligibility-api
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/eligibility-api/documentation/index.md

Overview: The Benefits Eligibility Service API offers features for third party vendors or merchants to verify benefits eligibility, create unique card identifiers, and generate access tokens. By using card numbers or identifiers, the Eligibility API confirms cardholder eligibility for specific Mastercard Benefits. The Eligibility API  caters to Mastercard Service Providers (vendors) that intend to utilize this service to retrieve benefits and insurance details specific to the cardholder’s PAN or specific to the cardholder’s BIN/product. Within the Benefits Eligibility Service are two sub-apis, the Eligibility API and the Access Token API. Integration of the Eligibility API component allows vendors to check eligibility of a card number for a service. The Access Token API component generates the actual authentication token.Why it matters: The Benefits Eligibility Service can be used for a plethora of different use cases. These include retrieving active benefits (can be specific to vendor, card, product, etc.). It can be used to retrieve products associated with a specific card number. Finally, it can be used to retrieve access tokens for the vendor website and retrieve a card identifier for a provided card number

### Enhanced Currency Conversion Calculator
serviceId: enhanced-currency-conversion-calculator
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/enhanced-currency-conversion-calculator/documentation/index.md

Overview: The Enhanced Currency Conversion Calculator is a subscription-based service for Mastercard issuers in the European Economic Area (EEA) to access Mastercard’s daily and historical currency conversion rates, as well as European Central Bank (ECB) reference rates. It supports building digital experiences where cardholders can view the applicable Mastercard and ECB rates, the calculated billing amount, and the percentage mark-up between the two rates, in compliance with EU Regulation 2019/518. Rates are published daily and are effective for 24 hours.nWhy it matters: nThis helps applications meet regulatory requirements, precisely determine conversion details and results for payment scenarios including a comparison with ECB Rates. It supports use cases such as regulatory disclosures, banking transparency, travel expense tracking, and international payments.

Keywords: Mastercard enhanced currency conversion rate,
ECB currency conversion rate API, Mastercard currency conversion rate comparison to ECB currency conversion rate API, API for international payments currency API for issuers in the European Economic Area - EEA

### Ethoca Alerts Merchant API
serviceId: ethoca-alerts-for-merchants
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/ethoca-alerts-for-merchants/documentation/index.md

Overview: Ethoca Alerts is a collaborative tool that connects issuers and merchants together to share fraud and dispute data, enabling a faster dispute resolution process that reduces the need for chargebacks altogether.
 
The service provides two core APIs. Alerts Push API (outbound) delivers disputes that were shared by issuers to a merchant’s endpoint. Alerts Outcome API (inbound) lets merchants send back outcomes and feedback—such as refunded or not settled—so issuers know the issue was addressed and do not raise a chargeback. The end-to-end flow includes alert delivery, merchant acknowledgement, outcome submission, and confirmations back to both parties. Documentation includes use cases, tutorials, and test data to help teams connect and validate their flows.

- [Ethoca Alerts for Merchants and Merchant Partners](https://developer.mastercard.com/ethoca-alerts-for-merchants/documentation/index.md)

### Confirmed Fraud API
serviceId: fld-fraud-submission
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/fld-fraud-submission/documentation/index.md

The Fraud and Loss Database (FLD) is our enterprise source of truth for fraud transactions. We provide multiple channels to submit and manage confirmed fraud records, including Global File Transfer for batch submissions, manual search and submission via Mastercard Connect, file uploads (complete and minimal), and a growing API channel. Using APIs brings automation, efficiency, easier integration, personalization, and adaptability as needs evolve.Through the API channel, issuers or their processors can submit, update, delete, confirm, and track the status of fraud records for both Mastercard-built and issuer-built transactions. Submissions are processed in real time: requests are validated, the underlying transaction is located in our repositories when applicable, details are stored, and an Audit Control Number (ACN) is returned for reference. This service streamlines fraud reporting workflows, reduces manual effort, and ensures timely, consistent updates to a centralized fraud dataset. We also provide clear use cases and defined test cases to help teams validate end-to-end flows across add, change, delete, confirm, and status operations.

### In Control for Commercial Payments
serviceId: iccp
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/iccp/documentation/index.md

In Control for Commercial Payments (ICCP) streamlines business transactions using oconfiguratble spending controls and enhanced data. It allow you to create and manage virtual card numbers (VCNs) that offer a flexible, simple, and secure way to pay, while capturing the data you need to reconcile payments later. Specifically, the ICCP API allows issuers, corporations, and payment service providers to perform the following processes: generating a unique card number for every transaction, setting transaction level controls, getting enhanced data, automating reconciliation, and controlling workflow.Why it matters: The ICCP API can be used to manage the underlying real cards that fund virtual card payments by allowing users to retrieve, update, and delete primary billing account information. Among other things, it has the following key use cases: creating a virtual card to pay an invoice, reconciling business to business (B2B) payments, updating virtual card, canceling virtual cards, obtaining entity identifiers, and managing real cards. The service provides resource discovery (real cards, purchase templates, suppliers) to streamline setup and governance of payments, and supports reconciliation through invoice addenda and reporting on VCN authorizations and clearings, including clearing control capabilities and related reports. ICCP helps accounts payable teams make fast, secure invoice payments, capture early payment discounts, and retain the credit benefits of card payments.

### Identity Insights for Transactions
serviceId: identity-insights-for-transactions
authType: MTLS
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/identity-insights-for-transactions/documentation/index.md

Identity Insights for Transactions is a risk intelligence service that delivers individual and device insights in a single payload to support safer, smoother card‑not‑present commerce. We address the challenge of balancing fraud prevention with customer experience by providing data and risk indicators that help you understand who the customer is and how their information is being used online, alongside device behavior signals to recognize trusted customers or flag suspicious activity. This helps reduce risk while enabling more legitimate transactions and long‑term customer value.The service can be used directly by merchants and acquirers or embedded by partners such as payment service providers and fraud platforms. It fits at multiple points in the transaction lifecycle—pre‑authentication, pre‑authorization, and post‑authorization—so teams can query insights and make informed risk decisions: proceed to authorization or authentication, decline, send for manual review, reverse, or continue to settlement/shipment. In PSD2 markets, acquirers can use these insights to help determine whether to apply TRA exemptions, route transactions for authentication, or decline, supporting optimized regulatory decisioning.

- [Identity Insights for Transactions](https://developer.mastercard.com/identity-insights-for-transactions/documentation/index.md)

### Locations
serviceId: locations-intelligence
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/locations-intelligence/documentation/index.md

Overview: Locations Intelligence provides access to our global database of locations for integration into apps and websites. It includes two APIs: ATM Locations for Mastercard’s global ATM dataset, and Merchant Locations for rePower and Mastercard easySavings participating merchant locations. The service is available in over 120 countries (see the documentation for the full list) and is designed for use by everyone.This service helps developers and product teams surface accurate, up-to-date location information so users can find nearby ATMs and participating merchant locations within the rePower and easySavings programs. Pricing is determined when requesting production access.

Keywords: Location Service, ATM Locations API, Merchant Locations API, Global location database, Mastercard easySavings, rePower merchant locations, Location discovery, Verified ATM location data, Participating merchant programmes

- [Location Intelligence Resources](https://developer.mastercard.com/locations-intelligence/documentation/index.md)

### Buyer Payment Agent
serviceId: mastercard-buyer-payment-agent
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercard-buyer-payment-agent/documentation/index.md

Overview:  Mastercard Buyer Payment Agent enables Buyer Payment Agents (such as banks, procure-to-pay networks, and AP automation providers) to act on behalf of Buyers to submit and manage payment instructions to suppliers through Business Payment Service. The service supports card and account-to-account (A2A) payment initiation, status updates, and retrieval of payment instruction details, with global availability and certain use cases restricted by region. It also provides optional supplier verification (retrieve supplier details) and a structured linking process between buyers and suppliers to ensure the correct entities transact. Why It Matters: This API helps reduce friction in B2B transactions by standardizing payment processes, improving reconciliation, and enabling real-time data exchange. It supports use cases such as automated invoice payments, supplier onboarding, and payment preference enforcement. By connecting to Mastercard’s open-loop network, Buyer Payment Agents can reach a broader supplier base and offer more flexible payment options.

- [Buyer Payment Agent](https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercard-buyer-payment-agent/documentation/index.md)

### Mastercard Processing Core
serviceId: mastercard-processing-core
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercard-processing-core/documentation/index.md

Overview: Mastercard Processing Core provides APIs to manage card programs and transaction lifecycles within our Card Management System (CMS). We register transactions as documents and expose rich retrieval and management capabilities, including posting internal debit and credit transactions, charging ad‑hoc fees (predefined during onboarding), reversing transactions, and releasing blocked funds for pending transactions. The service also enables retrieving transactions and associated documents using multiple identifiers (such as ARN, RRN, SRN, and transactionId), alongside detailed amounts, currencies, posting statuses, and processing metadata.Beyond transactions, we support operational data management and controls. You can retrieve account contracts for a client (with pagination), obtain contract tree structure summaries, and fetch usage limit details stored in the CMS. Configuration uses dictionaries to validate and transform values between the API and CMS, and to enforce data formats (for example, character sets and masks), while classifiers and parameters can be set or retrieved at client or contract level.

- [Mastercard Processing Core APIs](https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercard-processing-core/documentation/index.md)

### Supplier Payment Agent
serviceId: mastercard-supplier-payment-agent
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercard-supplier-payment-agent/documentation/index.md

Overview: Mastercard Supplier Payment Agent enables Supplier Payment Agents (such as banks, ERP automation agents, payment service providers) to act on behalf of Suppliers to retrieve and process payment instructions. We support managing both card and account-to-account (A2A) payment instructions, including updating instructions and retrieving instruction details and payment status. The service also facilitates linking between Suppliers and Buyers (e.g., nudging a Buyer Payment Agent to link, approving/rejecting requests, deleting links, and searching approved links). 

Why it matters: This API empowers Supplier Payment Agents to automate payment acceptance, streamline reconciliation, and enforce payment controls. It supports use cases such as supplier onboarding, payment tracking, and integration with commercial payment platforms. By leveraging Mastercard’s Business Payment Service, Supplier Payment Agents can offer suppliers a more efficient and scalable way to receive payments across diverse buyer networks.

### Mastercom
serviceId: mastercom
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercom/documentation/index.md

Overview: Mastercom is Mastercard’s dispute resolution platform that enables issuers, acquirers, and third parties to manage the full lifecycle of payment disputes. Through its APIs, Mastercom supports key dispute events such as Collaboration, chargebacks, second presentments, fee collections, and case filings. It organizes all dispute activities under a single claim ID, allowing for efficient tracking and resolution. Mastercom also facilitates secure document exchange, real-time notifications, and reporting to streamline dispute handling across the ecosystem. Why It Matters: Mastercom helps reduce the time, cost, and complexity of resolving payment disputes by automating workflows and consolidating dispute data. It enables faster resolution—often within 72 hours—compared to traditional manual processes that can take weeks. By providing a standardized, API-driven approach, Mastercom improves transparency, reduces chargeback processing costs, and ensures that financial responsibility is accurately assigned between issuers and acquirers. It is a critical tool for maintaining trust and operational efficiency in the global payments ecosystem.

Keywords: Mastercom, Dispute resolution API for issuers, Dispute resolution API for acquirers, Dispite Management, Chargeback Processing API for chargeback and dispute resolution automation

- [Mastercom](https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercom/documentation/index.md)
- [List of Violation Codes](https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercom/documentation/additional-resources/list-of-violation-codes/index.md)
  - [Image and Document Requirements](https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercom/documentation/additional-resources/image-and-document-requirements/index.md)

### Mastercom-Extended
serviceId: mastercom-extended
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mastercom-extended/documentation/index.md

Overview: Mastercom Extended is our next-generation API suite for cardholder dispute processing across the dispute resolution lifecycle. It enables issuers and acquirers to efficiently and securely query and update dispute data, with current capabilities focused on initiating disputes and first chargebacks for issuers, receiving chargebacks and creating representments for acquirers, and moving funds between parties through the Mastercard Switch. As Mastercom Extended expands to cover the full lifecycle, it will replace the existing Mastercom API. Why It Matters: Mastercom Extended helps reduce the time and complexity of resolving disputes by offering a modular, API-first approach to managing dispute workflows. Supported workflows include searching for transactions; creating and managing claims; initiating, updating, and reversing chargebacks; creating, acknowledging, and reversing representments; and managing reports, documents, and queues tied to chargebacks or representments. It also supports pre-arbitration, arbitration, pre-compliance, and compliance case filing (including related document management) and offers Send Payment dispute processing for Originating and Receiving Institutions.

Keywords: Mastercom Extended, Dispute resolution API for issuers, Dispute resolution API for acquirers, Dispute Management, Chargeback Processing API for chargeback and dispute resolution automation

### MATCH Pro
serviceId: match
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/match/documentation/index.md

Overview: MATCH Pro (Mastercard Alert To Control High-risk Merchants) is our mandatory system for Mastercard Acquirers to assess merchant risk before entering Merchant or Sponsored Merchant Agreements. It centralizes Authorized User–reported records of merchants (and their owners) terminated within the past five years, enabling an independent risk determination prior to onboarding and transaction submission. MATCH Pro provides automated, real-time access via APIs to support due diligence and ongoing risk review. Note: effective January 1, 2026, we will decommission legacy MATCH APIs and legacy MATCH bulk files through GFT; MATCH Pro will no longer support these files.Core functionality includes: submitting termination inquiries to identify exact or phonetic matches to previously terminated merchants; adding terminated merchants (with applicable reason codes) within five days, with immediate availability to other acquirers and a reference number confirmation; and retroactive inquiries that continuously monitor prior inquiries for 360 days and notify via email when a new possible match appears (retroactive matches are retained for 30 days and should be reviewed daily). Additional capabilities include retrieving inquiry history (past 365 days) and obtaining the originating acquirer’s contact details for further investigation, along with reference data for required address fields (countries, states, cities). By reducing due diligence and post-termination processing from a day to real-time responses, MATCH Pro helps acquirers efficiently identify and manage high-risk merchants. For broader merchant risk analysis, we also offer Onboarding Risk Check to assess cyber risk, sanctions exposure, and business identity verification.

- [MATCH Pro](https://developer.mastercard.com/match/documentation/index.md)
- [API Basics](https://developer.mastercard.com/match/documentation/api-basics/index.md)
- [API Reference](https://developer.mastercard.com/match/documentation/api-reference/index.md)
- [Code and Formats](https://developer.mastercard.com/match/documentation/code-and-formats/index.md)
- [Postman Collection](https://developer.mastercard.com/match/documentation/postman-collection/index.md)

### MDES Customer Service
serviceId: mdes-customer-service
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-customer-service/documentation/index.md

Overview: MDES Customer Service is a set of APIs that enable issuers onboarded to MDES to manage the lifecycle of their own tokens and integrate tokenized information into their customer service interfaces. This service supports core servicing functions such as searching for tokens by PAN or Token Unique Reference (TUR), activating tokens, retrieving and resending activation methods, managing token states (suspend, unsuspend, delete, update), and viewing token status history. Additional capabilities include retrieving token-related transactions, submitting token comments, resetting mobile PINs, updating token assurance levels, disabling payment channels, and searching by token requestor.nnWhy it matters: These APIs enable issuers and customer service teams to efficiently resolve common servicing scenarios involving digital cards, such as reactivating tokens after device recovery, maintaining token continuity during PAN updates, and managing token identifiers during product migrations. The service is designed with privacy and compliance in mind—only partial PANs and token references are exposed, all traffic is encrypted using TLS 1.2, and the APIs are PCI DSS compliant. Token search supports pagination for large datasets, and transaction retrieval is limited to the past 30 days to support fraud investigations and customer inquiries. Issuers can test integrations in the MTF environment, and a web-based Customer Service portal is also available for operational support.

### MDES Digital Enablement API
serviceId: mdes-digital-enablement
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-digital-enablement/documentation/index.md

Overview: The MDES Digital Enablement API is a Mastercard service that allows merchants, wallet providers, and other token requestors to tokenize cardholder PANs and manage the lifecycle of digital tokens for secure e-commerce and in-app transactions. It supports token creation, transaction cryptogram generation for Digital Secure Remote Payment (DSRP), token suspension and unsuspension, asset retrieval, and outbound notifications for token updates. The API is designed to work with Mastercard and Maestro cards, and supports both direct merchant integrations and “on-behalf-of” models via payment service providers. It also enables push provisioning through Token Connect, allowing consumers to securely transfer tokenized payment credentials from their issuer’s app to merchant platforms.Why It Matters: The MDES Digital Enablement API plays a critical role in enabling secure, scalable, and convenient digital payments. By replacing sensitive card data with tokens, it reduces the risk of fraud and protects cardholder information across digital channels. It supports seamless checkout experiences, improves token lifecycle management, and enables merchants to comply with security standards like PCI DSS. The API is encrypted end-to-end, supports OAuth 1.0a authentication, and ensures sensitive data is protected using Mastercard’s encryption protocols. It is a foundational component of Mastercard’s tokenization ecosystem, helping partners accelerate time to market and expand digital payment capabilities.

Keywords: Digital Enablement API, M4M API, MDES For Merchants, Digital Enablement for Merchants, Merchant Tokenization

### MDES Pre-digitization
serviceId: mdes-pre-digitization
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-pre-digitization/documentation/index.md

Overview: MDES Pre-digitization is part of the Mastercard Digital Enablement Service (MDES), which replaces primary account numbers (PANs) with secure digital payment tokens for contactless, in-app, and card-on-file transactions. This service covers the processes required before a token is ready for use, enabling issuers to authorize token creation, authenticate account holders, and manage activation methods and code delivery. It supports both device provisioning and merchant-initiated tokenization, and is available via ISO network messages configured in MDES Manager or through web services that notify issuers of tokenization requests made by or on behalf of their cardholders.Why It Matters: MDES Pre-digitization allows issuers to guide the wallet experience and maintain control over token provisioning, even when working with third-party processors that lack native digital capabilities. It supports flexible implementation models—fully web service-based or hybrid—and reduces the effort, cost, and time required to adopt digital payments. Common use cases include token requestor-initiated tokenization and issuer-initiated tokenization, helping issuers deliver secure, seamless digital payment experiences while minimizing network message maintenance.

Keywords: Pre-Digitization, Post-Tokenization Authentication, Authorize Service, Notify Token Updated (NTU), Tokenization, Provisioning

- [MDES Pre-Digitization](https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-pre-digitization/documentation/index.md)
- [API Basics](https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-pre-digitization/documentation/api-basics/index.md)
  - [Access to DigiCert Trusted Root Authority Certificates](https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-pre-digitization/documentation/api-basics/entrust/index.md)
  - [API Design Principles](https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-pre-digitization/documentation/api-basics/design_principles/index.md)

### MDES Token Connect
serviceId: mdes-token-connect
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-token-connect/documentation/index.md

Overview: MDES Token Connect enables Mastercard issuers to securely push payment card and account details to eligible token requestors—such as digital wallets, merchants, and commerce platforms—to initiate token creation. It supports issuer-initiated digitization from mobile banking apps or online banking platforms, allowing cardholders to provision their accounts instantly and securely. Core services include retrieving a list of enabled token requestors with metadata and logos, initiating push provisioning for one or multiple accounts, and generating a PushAccountReceipt with redirect URIs for token requestors to complete tokenization.nWhy It Matters: MDES Token Connect solves the challenge of fragmented, proprietary integrations by offering a scalable and interoperable framework. Once connected, issuers can reach any participating MDES token requestor through a single integration. This improves the cardholder experience by enabling instant provisioning, reducing manual entry, and allowing immediate use of tokenized credentials across digital wallets and merchant platforms. The service supports flows where consumers are redirected to token requestors to complete tokenization, and when needed, additional authentication can be performed via the consent service

Keywords: Push Provisioning, TAV, Redirect, Proprietary Communication, Pull Provisioning 

- [MDES Token Connect](https://developer.mastercard.com/mdes-token-connect/documentation/index.md)

### Merchant Self Services
serviceId: merchant-self-services
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/merchant-self-services/documentation/index.md

The Merchant Self Services API lets a user provide merchant details and configurations for their sub-merchants to help onboard as quickly as possible for Consumer Clarity or Alerts. Consumer Clarity lets a merchant user provide issuers and cardholders with real-time information about their company. The data provided helps reduce cardholder disputes for transactions that they don't recognize and first-party misuse that occurs when a consumer knowingly makes a purchase for goods or services and then disputes it as fraudulent. Alerts makes it easier for businesses to share and action data to help reduce chargebacks.
Why it matters: Primarily used for onboarding/creating merchants/sub-merchants and updating their data, Merchant Self Services' core functionality focuses on creating, retrieving, updating, and deleting merchant descriptors and card acceptor names at the sub-merchant location level, supporting use cases across Consumer Clarity and Alerts.

Keywords: Onboarding, integration, APIs 

- [Ethoca Merchant Self Services](https://developer.mastercard.com/merchant-self-services/documentation/index.md)

### Open Finance US
serviceId: open-finance-us
authType: OpenBanking
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/open-finance-us/documentation/index.md

Overview: Open Finance US is our suite of API-based services that enables secure, consumer-permissioned access to financial data and decisioning to power payments, money management, and lending use cases. Core capabilities include account aggregation with tiered data access, real-time or cached account balances, account owner verification with optional identity insights, transaction data enrichment, and bank account validation via microdeposits (Account Validation Assistant). We also provide predictive risk tools such as Payment Success Indicator for assessing NSF risk and Consumer Foresight for spend insights, benchmarking, and forecasting, alongside lending analytics and reports.

The service helps reduce payment risk and improve approvals by combining real-time balance checks with predictive NSF scoring and explanatory attributes (PSI), and by validating routing/account details when users can’t or won’t link accounts (AVA). For lending, we evaluate account balances and cash flows, generate Payment History and Statement reports, and support small and medium business credit assessment. For personalization and PFM, Consumer Foresight delivers category-level historical trends, peer benchmarks, and future spend forecasts. Operationally, Client Hub surfaces financial institution connection health and invoice history, while Connect customization and UX guidance help deliver accessible, user-friendly consent and linking experiences. Note: some capabilities are premium or billable (for example, Get Available Balance live/cached, Consumer Foresight, and Statement Report), and PSI is for non-FCRA use cases with an FCRA variant available.

### Currency Conversion for Originating Institution
serviceId: payment-currency-conversion
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/payment-currency-conversion/documentation/index.md

The Currency Conversion Calculator for Originating Institution Reconciliation API enables Originating institutions to instantly access daily currency conversion rates for Mastercard Send cross-border payment transactions to Mastercard cards. This helps them determine the funds needed to reconcile with Mastercard before initiating a cross-border payment transactions. The currency conversion API provides a list of currencies for which Mastercard publish rates daily, the conversion rate statuses that have been published for a given data, and payment details based on the conversion rates (calculated settlement, transaction amount and currency, settlement currency, etc.).Why it matters: Primarily meant for originating institutions, the currency conversion calculator for originating institution reconciliation API calls for each cross-border payment transaction where the transaction currency differs from the settlement currency. The API response provides the currency conversion rate and settlement amount. This has a variety of use cases, primarily involving situations where originating institutions need conversion and currency details for transactions. 

- [Currency Conversion Calculator for Originating Institution Reconciliation](https://developer.mastercard.com/payment-currency-conversion/documentation/index.md)

### Offers for Publishers 
serviceId: presentment
authType: OAuth1.0a and OAuth2 (FAPI 2.0)
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/presentment/documentation/index.md

The Mastercard Offers for Publishers API provides a set of calls for Publishers to implement an offers program in their digital channel. As a result, the offers programs can drive top-of-wallet preference, promote spending, and increase engagement with merchant-funded offers. The API provides endpoints for offer presentment, helping Publishers manage an end-to-end offers program across digital channels, including websites and mobile apps, using all capabilities through the offers for publishers product. Why it matters: Primarily used by patterns and publishers, offers for publishers provide APIs that support core functionalities across user account administration and offer presentment. These functionalities include the following: user account administration to enable publishers to enroll cardholders into an offers program, presentment for websites and mobile applications, cardholder enrollment, account updates, card replacements, and user/account lookup using publisher-provided identifiers.

### Priceless Specials
serviceId: priceless-specials
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/priceless-specials/documentation/index.md

The Priceless Specials API provides a single platform where issuers, merchants, and cardholders can easily explore Mastercard offers and benefits from around the globe. Its key features include real-time offers that can be targeted for mass market and different cardholder segments.  These are exclusive for Mastercard cardholders. Priceless offers a readily curated list of featured programs and offers that can be filtered by location and category to be relevant to the users. These offers include key details like card product, offer eligibility, terms and conditions, etc.\n
Why it matters: Targeted towards issuers and strategic partners, the Priceless Specials API can be used to support several key use cases including retrieving offers, programs, benefits, languages, categories, countries, MC products, and merchant information.  Priceless Specials helps issuers enrich their digital channels with relevant, up-to-date offers and benefits, improving cardholder engagement. It centralizes discovery and retrieval of offers, benefits, programs, and related metadata, enabling filtering and presentation tailored to the issuer’s user experience. 

- [Priceless Specials](https://developer.mastercard.com/priceless-specials/documentation/index.md)
- [API Basics](https://developer.mastercard.com/priceless-specials/documentation/api-basics/index.md)
- [API Reference](https://developer.mastercard.com/priceless-specials/documentation/api-reference/index.md)
- [Code and Formats](https://developer.mastercard.com/priceless-specials/documentation/code-and-formats/index.md)

### RPPS Biller Maintenance Service
serviceId: rpps-biller-maintenance-service
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/rpps-biller-maintenance-service/documentation/index.md

This service will allow a Concentrators the ability to add billers in RPPS.

- [Remote Payment and Presentment Service (RPPS) Biller Maintenance Service](https://developer.mastercard.com/rpps-biller-maintenance-service/documentation/index.md)
- [API Basics](https://developer.mastercard.com/rpps-biller-maintenance-service/documentation/api-basics/index.md)
- [API Reference](https://developer.mastercard.com/rpps-biller-maintenance-service/documentation/api-reference/index.md)
- [Code and Formats](https://developer.mastercard.com/rpps-biller-maintenance-service/documentation/code-and-formats/index.md)

### Token Requestor Identifier API
serviceId: token-requestor-identifier-api
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/token-requestor-identifier-api/documentation/index.md

Overview: The Token Requestor Identifier (TRID) API is part of Mastercard’s Digital Enablement Service (MDES) and enables On-Behalf-Of Token Requestors (OBOTRs)—typically Payment Service Providers (PSPs)—to onboard and manage token requestors such as merchants or commerce platforms. The API allows OBOTRs to register token requestors in batches, update their details, and receive a unique Token Requestor Identifier (TRID) for each onboarded entity. This TRID is then used to tokenize card credentials via the MDES Tokenize API. The service supports streamlined onboarding, duplicate TRID detection, and eliminates manual errors through automation.Why It Matters: Token Requestor Identifier API helps PSPs operating in the On-Behalf-Of model manage token requestors across diverse merchant portfolios efficiently. It simplifies integration by offering a single interface for both creation and updates, reduces operational overhead, and ensures accurate token domain mapping. By enabling fast and secure onboarding, the API helps PSPs accelerate time to market for tokenized payment solutions and maintain consistency across digital commerce environments.

- [Token Requestor Identifier (TRID) API](https://developer.mastercard.com/token-requestor-identifier-api/documentation/index.md)

### Track Search
serviceId: track-search
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/track-search/documentation/index.md

Mastercard Track Search provides valuable Mastercard data to help better understand the multi-rail payment preferences of trading partners. Track search matches from Mastercard's merchant acceptance and Track Business Payment Service database to return key insights on supplier data, transaction message data, transaction recency data, etc. Primarily used by issuers, corporates, or Business Payment Service payment agents to increase efficiencies of commercial payment programs, track search provides access to key data from requested corporates for optimized commercial payment program planning.Why it matters: This matters for teams that need efficient, compliant partner discovery and verification across large datasets. By handling bulk requests and asynchronous completion, Track Search reduces manual lookups and operational friction, while ensuring searches are performed by the right parties under clear authorization rules. It can be used to initiate bulk search, check status, and retrieve results.

### Transaction Notifications
serviceId: transaction-notifications
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/transaction-notifications/documentation/index.md

Overview: Transaction Notifications is a powerful API that delivers real-time transaction data , including both authorization and clearing events, to applications with enrolled cardholders, once appropriate consent is obtained. It supports both bulk enrollment (via issuers, corporate admins, or fintech partners) and individual enrollment, where cardholders authenticate directly with the application.Once a card is enrolled, applications receive timely, context-rich notifications for eligible card activity, enabling dynamic engagement and personalized experiences.Why Use Transaction Notifications?Whether you're building a budgeting app, fraud alert system, loyalty platform, or personalized rewards experience, Transaction Notifications gives you the ability to react instantly to cardholder activity. This enables:Real-time engagement: Trigger contextual messages, offers, or insights the moment a transaction occurs.Enhanced user trust: Provide transparency and security through instant alerts and confirmations.Smarter personalization: Use transaction data to tailor experiences, recommendations, and financial insights.Developer-Friendly Features Transaction Notifications is built with developers in mind, offering:Hosted Web UI: For applications that prefer not to handle PCI data directly, we offer a secure, Mastercard-hosted web interface for cardholder enrollment and consent capture. Reliable delivery: Automatic retries for failed webhooks over a 24-hour window, with progressive backoff. Resilience: Undelivered notifications are stored for up to 7 days and can be retrieved via a dedicated API.Flexibility: Support for multiple webhook endpoints and customizable payloads to meet data separation and minimization requirements.

Keywords: Real-time transaction data, Cardholder purchase notifications, Expense management automation, Credit card transaction notification, Network transaction data, Personalized financial experiences

### User Account Management
serviceId: user-account-management-service
authType: OAuth1.0a
sourceUrl: https://developer.mastercard.com/user-account-management-service/documentation/index.md

Perform user enrollments or updates to profile details.
